  Josef又搬了一次房，箱子里多了一把缺齿的梳子和一叠没寄出的信。新住处离电车线更远，邻居说德语很快；他只能点头，等对方走后再把门关上，把今天听错的词一个一个记在旧票据背面。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 24 -> 25; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 11 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 63 -> 67; cash -1 (the move and a new rent deposit（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

- **1953.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Josef的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price（其中25克朗记入债务）); health 0.04 (quinine obtained and taken); condition; role; knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1953.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef把通知念给Friedrich Huber听，Friedrich Huber摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1953.09** `r1-cul.church-charity::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/refused-without-certificate` tags: faith, charity, church, hunger, rules, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Stein让Josef先查领汤者的洗礼证，没有证的只能领面包。一个男人把证拍在桌上，说自己在战壕里丢的；Josef看了看Gustav Stein，把汤勺放下了。那天剩下的汤被倒进沟里，热气在雪上化出一个小洞。
  _effects: condition; flag +refused_soup_without_certificate; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-134; knowledge “the parish requires a baptism certificate for soup”_

- **1953.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Friedrich Huber从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1954.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Friedrich Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Friedrich Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015240_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa Huber把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Josef在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Josef Bauer 2分给Josef半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”_

- **1954.05** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/refuse-push-bill` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef把药费单从糖罐底下抽出来，对折，推回桌子中央，说上个月的工钱已经贴过煤钱，这回一个赫勒也拿不出。Anna Bauer的脸沉下来，练习本翻得哗哗响，最后把单子重新压回糖罐底下，起身去够门后的外套。三只咖啡杯里的渣子凉在桌上，兄妹这一回把话都说薄了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.05** `r15-love.borrowed-room::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/begins` tags: love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef和Mitzi Hofer 2开始在咖啡馆后门见面。每次只有半个钟头，说的都是不能在家里说的话；Josef回家前会在街角站一会儿，把围巾重新系好，确认身上没有别人的烟味。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:452; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +secret_attachment_active_

- **1954.06** `fa17-studio.private-failure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/borrow-a-paragraph` tags: art, writing, failure, poverty, privacy, craft, plagiarism, vienna
  Josef从一九〇四年的画报上抄下一段描写雨夜的话，把地名和人名换掉，寄给一家征文比赛。抄的时候，Josef告诉自己只借这一小段；寄出去之后，却在邮局门口站了很久，反复回想那段话里有没有一个词是别人一眼就能认出来的。
  _effects: state_add fa17-plagiarist state:world-vienna-1912:451; skill; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +borrowed_paragraph_

- **1954.06** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Josef把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Josef没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 9 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 25 -> 26; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015987_

- **1954.08** `r2-law.mortgage-foreclosure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-to-grain-merchant` tags: finance, mortgage, auction, housing, court, vienna, urban, postwar
  槌子落在第七号，Josef的院子归了一个外地来的粮商。Emil Bauer 4让Josef在十五天内搬走，Rosa Huber把清单念到“灶台一座”时停了一下。Grete Stein 3把自家的板车推来，说先装要紧的，家具以后再说。
  _effects: cash -10 (拍卖槌落在第七号（其中5克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +home_sold_at_auction; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153; knowledge “the auction buyer is a grain merchant from outside the distr”_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/another-rejection` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  画店老板把Josef的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Josef抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 43 -> 50; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 8 -> 7; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 32 -> 29_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Josef能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 26 -> 30; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 12 -> 17; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 67 -> 65; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:fa17-plagiarist/the-borrowed-year/someone-quotes-the-source` tags: fa17-plagiarist, art, plagiarism, secret, exposure, guilt, reputation, debt
  一位中学教师在读者来信里引用了同一段话，注明出处是十年前的《星期日报》。编辑把两段并排放在文化版上，问Josef是否愿意解释；Josef盯着那两栏字，发现连标点都几乎没有改。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.exposure 10 -> 22; state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.guilt 25 -> 33; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Josef没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 10 -> 25; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 5 -> 10; reputation -0.04000000000000001_

- **1955.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-benefit-office/sent-to-relief-work` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Winter说补助金只发给有家属的人，Josef单身，只能去修路队领工钱。第二天Josef扛着铁锹站在城郊，和几十个男人一起把碎砖填进车辙。工钱按日结，比补助多两克朗，但一天下来，手掌磨出了三个水泡。
  _effects: cash 2 (补助金窗口的第二张表); condition; flag +work13_sent_to_relief_work_

- **1955.06** `r1-hc.assault-knife::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/knife-fight-intervene` tags: street, assault, knife, violence, police, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef没有动手，把Grete Lechner 2推到身后，又伸手按住Friedrich Lechner拿刀的手腕。刀掉在地上，谁也没去捡；Berta Winter来时，Friedrich Lechner说只是闹着玩，Josef看着自己发抖的手，没说话。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +stopped_knife_fight; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child; knowledge “the knife was on the ground before the police arrived”_

- **1955.06** `r13-turn.taking-in-orphan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-housing-crowding/relative-came-forward` tags: family, orphan, care, household, scarcity, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Hofer打听到孩子还有一个姑母在城外，Josef陪孩子坐了两个钟头的火车去认亲。姑母开门时先看孩子的鞋，再看Josef的脸。孩子留下了，Josef回程时把带来的那包衣服又拎了回来，觉得轻得不像话。
  _effects: cash -2 (门口多出来的一双鞋（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +orphan_sent_to_relative; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008750:child; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1955.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Grete Lechner 2把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Josef说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Mitzi在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Josef的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148; reputation -0.07_

- **1956.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Friedrich Lechner 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Lechner 2在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154_

- **1956.01** `fa17-press.plagiarism-accusation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/counter-accusation` tags: art, plagiarism, accusation, media, scandal, reputation, law, vienna
  Josef没有否认，而是翻出Ferdinand Lechner三年前发表的一篇随笔，指出其中一段和某本旅行手册几乎一样。Emil Bauer 4把两件事并排做成“文坛互指”的版面；读者更爱看两个人互相撕，至于谁先抄了谁，反而没人再追问。Ferdinand Lechner的名字从此在编辑部里变成了一个笑话。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.exposure 22 -> 16; state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.guilt 33 -> 38; reputation 0.03; reputation -0.08000000000000002; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; flag +counter_accusation_

- **1956.01** `state_expire:mb17-newcomer` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Josef的「初到维也纳的人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:384; flag -mb17_newcomer_active_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:fa17-plagiarist/the-borrowed-year/someone-quotes-the-source` tags: fa17-plagiarist, art, plagiarism, secret, exposure, guilt, reputation, debt
  一位中学教师在读者来信里引用了同一段话，注明出处是十年前的《星期日报》。编辑把两段并排放在文化版上，问Josef是否愿意解释；Josef盯着那两栏字，发现连标点都几乎没有改。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.exposure 16 -> 28; state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.guilt 38 -> 46; reputation -0.03_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Josef没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 25 -> 40; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 10 -> 15; reputation -0.04000000000000001_

- **1956.01** `r13-work.collect-debt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrote-it-off` tags: commerce, debt, collection, neighbor, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在门口站了一会儿，看见Elisabeth Kraus家的孩子在楼梯上啃面包皮。欠条没有拿出来，Josef只说下个月再说。回家的路上，Katharina Bauer 2问这笔账还记不记，Josef说记着，但不催了；账本上那一行被折了一个角。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001253:child; knowledge “the debtor’s household has no cash to seize”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1956.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef对Helene Huber说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Helene Huber把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Josef的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005069:child_

- **1956.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/ledger-on-table` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Elisabeth Bauer的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Josef在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1957.01** `r15-love.jealous-rival::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/rival-warns` tags: love, jealousy, rival, secret, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Kral在巷口拦住Josef，说已经看见过三次，还知道Josef把信藏在哪本书里。Ferdinand Kral没有提要求，只让Josef“小心一点”；Josef回到家，把那本书从书架上取下来，塞进了煤箱。
  _effects: state_add jealous-rival state:world-vienna-1912:506; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 15 -> 30; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 40 -> 45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +jealous_rival_active_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Josef把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Josef把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 53 -> 57; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 7 -> 10; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 50 -> 48; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:fa17-plagiarist/the-borrowed-year/nobody-noticed` tags: fa17-plagiarist, art, plagiarism, secret, exposure, guilt, reputation, debt
  又一年过去，没有人把那段话和旧杂志联系起来。Josef在读者来信里看见有人夸那句“像从生活里直接捞出来的”，回信时写得很短；夜里，Josef把原刊从书架最上层取下来，又放回去。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.exposure 28 -> 24; state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.guilt 46 -> 51; state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.borrowed 45 -> 48; cash 1 (新作品的稿费)_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/still-hidden` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Josef在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 45 -> 40; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 55 -> 58; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `r13-civ.ask-pharmacist::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/double-dose-caught` tags: health, pharmacy, advice, dosage, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton Weiss 2发现处方上写着一天三次，可瓶签上是一天六次，拿铅笔把“六”划掉，让Josef回去找医生重签。Josef问会不会得罪人，Anton Weiss 2说吃错药的人不会来道谢。
  _effects: cash -2 (柜台后面的小秤（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +pharmacist_caught_overdose; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; knowledge “the bottle label doubled the prescribed dose”_

- **1957.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Josef摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Franz Stein第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Josef只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1957.03** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Franz Stein把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Franz Stein在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。

### 180 subject events

- **1914.04** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Lechner-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.09** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1916.06** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/old-shoes` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  鞋底补过两层，针脚很粗，鞋头磨得发白。Rosa把脚塞进去，脚趾顶到前头，只能蜷起来。Alois Lechner问紧不紧，Rosa说正好。Alois Lechner蹲下按了按鞋帮，说明年脚再长就放不下了，把针线盒从桌上挪开。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; health -0.020000000000000018 (鞋挤脚走路发疼)_

- **1918.01** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  碗里倒上醋，兑一半凉水，Alois Lechner把布巾浸透，拧到不滴水，敷在Rosa的额头上。布一热就换，换下来的搭在床沿。Rosa半夜说梦话，Alois Lechner用手背试了试温度，又去换一盆水。天亮时枕布湿了一片，Alois Lechner的手指白得像泡过石灰。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  _effects: food; health -0.02 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1930.09** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social

  _effects: food; reputation -0.15; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

  区公所的长凳坐满了人。Berta Kral的笔尖在名册上划了一道，把纸推过来时没有抬眼。Rosa把纸折了三折，塞进Berta Hofer手里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1930.12** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; flag +rent_installment_plan_

  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Rosa把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -1 (慈善会登记簿上的空行（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.04; flag +joined_charity_visiting_round; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; role; flag +stove_schedule; knowledge “灶台一三五归东屋，二四六归西屋，星期日谁先起谁用。”_

  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Rosa看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。

  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

  Rosa在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个男婴，取名Gustav 2。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Josef Berg 2把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Rosa躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:201_

- **1938.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  天色还灰着，Rosa手里攥着几张折皱的证明。Berta Weiss 2让他念车皮上的号码，听见念对了，就朝装卸班一挥手，说先按日结钱，一天两克朗四十分。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:003638; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Rosa和Josef Berg 2把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood -0.040000000000000036; mood -0.07999999999999999; cash -3 (separate household goods（其中3克朗记入债务）)_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.apologize::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/apology-rejected` tags: task, social, apology, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa把带来的半块肥皂放在窗台上，说知道不够。Alois Berg没有碰肥皂，只说那天在场的人都记得Rosa笑的样子。Rosa站到腿发麻，最后把肥皂收回口袋，转身时听见窗子从里面关上了。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1939.04** `r2-leisure.secret-society::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::secret-society-crisis/suspected-an-infiltrator` tags: leisure, secret_society, conspiracy, police, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  危机那年，名单上多了一个不认识的代号“燕子”。Klara说这人是新来的，Berta Weiss 2说在警察局门口见过他。Rosa把名单摊在灯下，发现“燕子”的笔迹和Franz Moser的登记表很像。阁楼下面传来三下慢、两下快的敲门声。
  _effects: condition; flag +suspected_society_infiltrator; relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “the new member codenamed Swallow may be a police agent”_

- **1939.12** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Rosa寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Rosa追到码头时，Josef Bauer已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Rosa在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-josef-bauer; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition_

- **1939.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Rosa在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。
  _effects: condition; food_

- **1940.01** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/queue-until-noon` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Rosa在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Rosa去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Rosa数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1940.01** `r13-civ.ration-card::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/queue-closed` tags: politics, rationing, food, war, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa排到窗口时，Alois Berg把木牌翻成“今日止”，说本区的配额发完了，明天再来。队伍没有散，有人开始数前面还有多少人。Rosa把身份证攥在手里，站到腿发麻。
  _effects: flag +ration_queue_closed; knowledge “the district quota can run out before the window closes”_

- **1940.04** `r13-turn.political-arrest::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/taken-in-the-night` tags: politics, arrest, repression, union, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  敲门声很轻，开了门却站着四个人。Alois Berg把搜查令举到Rosa眼前，说只看印章，不用看字。Klara在对面门缝里看着，Rosa被带走时，桌上那本工会手册还翻开着。第二天，厂里只说Rosa没来上工。
  _effects: cash -4 (夜里敲门的便衣（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +arrested_for_political_activity; role; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the arrest was made under a warrant the subject could not re”; unemployment (夜里敲门的便衣)_

- **1940.09** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Rosa的裙角。Grete Stein 2的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Rosa咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1940.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Rosa靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1941.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007152_

- **1941.01** `r14-work.claim-unemployment::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/benefit-approved` tags: work, unemployment_benefit, institution, poverty, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Moser把号码牌从窗口推出来，让Rosa去走廊尽头等。轮到Rosa时，Franz Moser问最后一份工做了多久，又让Rosa把解雇的纸拿出来。Rosa只有一张白信封，没有理由。Franz Moser看了半晌，还是在表上写了“准予”，每月六克朗。
  _effects: cash 6 (失业救济的号码牌); condition; flag +unemployment_benefit; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “six kronen a month is granted without a written reason for d”_

- **1941.05** `r2-law.pawn-ticket::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ticket-sold` tags: finance, pawnshop, debt, household, loss, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa把当票卖给Emil Moser 2，换了几天的菜钱。Emil Moser 2说等发了工钱就去赎，赎出来再卖给Rosa，价钱另算。Friedrich Hofer 2在柜台后听见，说当票认票不认人；Rosa把票递过去时，手指在票角上多按了一下。
  _effects: cash 4 (过期的当票); condition; flag +sold_pawn_ticket_to_neighbor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; knowledge “a pawn ticket can be redeemed by anyone holding it”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1941.05** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1941.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1941.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1941.12** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/small-part` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, vienna
  Emil Hofer只给了Rosa三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Rosa把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Rosa，但乐队指挥记住了Rosa每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:242; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); role; flag +debut_small_part; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:243_

  Franz Moser说夫妻不同国籍要分开登记，Katharina Berg被分到铁丝网另一侧。Rosa隔着两道网把一块面包递过去，Katharina Berg没接住，面包落在泥里。Franz Moser吹哨让所有人回棚，Rosa看见Katharina Berg的手还伸在网眼上。
  _effects: condition; flag +partner_separated_by_nationality; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000437:child; knowledge “the camp separates married couples of different nationality”_

- **1942.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Elisabeth Berg排在前面，回头替Rosa看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Rosa用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000299:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Friedrich Lechner低声说下次带点烟丝，藏在面包里。Rosa没有答应，走的时候把面包掰开给Luise Stein看；Friedrich Lechner隔着桌子看着，眼神像小时候被抢了糖，嘴唇动了一下，没有出声。
  _effects: cash -2 (探监室的长桌); flag +refused_contraband_for_prisoner; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; knowledge “the prisoner asked for tobacco in the next bread”_

  画店老板把Rosa的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Rosa抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 55 -> 62; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 12 -> 11; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 65 -> 62; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/absorbed-again` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  画室的门一关，时间就又变成Emil Hofer的。Rosa坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Rosa发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 35 -> 41; state_parameter fa17-muse.inspiration 50 -> 52; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 10 -> 8_

- **1942.01** `kernel-family.inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/inheritance.older-will` tags: task, task:family, family, inheritance, siblings, estate, law, vienna
  Mitzi从行李里掏出另一张更早的遗嘱，说工具和存款都该归长子。Franz Moser把两张纸并排放好，说私人纠纷要去法院；诉讼费三十克朗，Rosa拿不出。遗产在办事处冻结了半年，Rosa靠替人浆洗撑过冬天。
  _effects: cash -3 (copies, stamps and travel to the inheritance office); role; flag +inheritance_frozen; knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷。”_

- **1942.10** `r2-law.will-reading::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/settled-quietly` tags: law, inheritance, will, family, probate, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa没有争，把遗嘱交给Luise Stein存档，自己只拿了父亲的一只烟斗。Friedrich Lechner后来把房子卖了，钱分了一半送来。Klara说这院子以后没梨吃了；Rosa把烟斗点上，说树还在别人家院子里。
  _effects: cash 8 (遗嘱第三页被水泡过); flag +settled_inheritance_without_dispute; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the house was sold and the proceeds split in half”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1943.01** `r14-soc.courtyard-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/seen-on-the-stairs` tags: rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, shame, gender, vienna, urban, crisis
  洗衣房里，Emil Hofer把声音压得很低，说昨夜看见Rosa从三楼那个有妇之夫的门口出来，鞋跟还在楼梯上敲了两下。水盆里的床单拧成一股。Rosa站在门口，手里还提着刚买的盐。水盆里的水溅到Rosa的鞋面上，Emil Hofer没有再抬头。水盆被端走时，鞋底在湿地上留下一串黑印。
  _effects: reputation -0.11999999999999998; condition; flag +rumor_target; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。

  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Rosa当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000299:child_

  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child_

- **1943.11** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Klara手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Klara把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Rosa记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 42 -> 46; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 11 -> 14; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 62 -> 60; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa在车站长椅上认出Friedrich Lechner，对方正把车票捏在手里。两人隔着一个人的空位坐下，先说了几句天气，Rosa才问那年为什么没有来送葬。Friedrich Lechner说车票买了，站台上又折回去；说到一半，两个人都看着铁轨，谁也没有再追问。
  _effects: flag +reconciled_with_estranged_sibling; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child; knowledge “the sibling bought a ticket to the funeral but turned back”_

- **1944.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把Grete Moser和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Rosa把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1944.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.church-bell` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Rosa没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1944.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa和Alois Moser 3一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Rosa去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Rosa先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Rosa看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010225_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Rosa躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Rosa去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `state_expire:fa17-muse` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  Rosa的「画布上的人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:243_

- **1945.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.pay-deposit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Rosa从内袋里数出两克朗压在柜台上，Rosa Huber翻开借书卡，用铅笔把上次迟还的四天圈出来，还是把小说递了过来。Rosa把书塞进外套里侧，出门就翻到第一页，边走边读，差点撞上推手推车的菜贩；两克朗换来的不只是一本小说，还有一整个下午的安静。
  _effects: cash -2 (租书店借小说的押金); skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1945.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Friedrich Hofer嫌Rosa的床单滴水滴到Friedrich Hofer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Rosa下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Rosa把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Friedrich Hofer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1945.11** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; reputation -0.03; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/another-rejection` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  画店老板把Rosa的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Rosa抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 60 -> 67; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 14 -> 13; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 62 -> 59; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  市政厅办起了工人夜校，Rosa替Alois Lechner去报名。管登记的人问年龄，Rosa报小了五岁；Alois Lechner在旁边扯她的袖子，最后自己说“四十七”。第一晚回家，老人把发的铅笔用布包好，说老师夸Alois Lechner握笔稳。
  _effects: cash -1 (在厨房桌上写自己的名字); flag +enrolled_parent_in_night_school; role; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the adult night school takes workers after the evening shift”; skill; condition_

- **1946.02** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Rosa跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Elisabeth Kraus探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001253:child_

- **1946.02** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Hermann Kral 2敲了门。Rosa掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Hermann Kral 2什么也没说就走了。夜里Rosa听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1946.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Mitzi在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Mitzi敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Maria Winter 2在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Rosa把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1947.01** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-name-taken` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  门房把Rosa的名字写进本子，说第二天带身份证件和住址证明来，录不录用还得看Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的意思。Rosa走出几步又回头，问清要几张证明，门房不耐烦地敲了敲玻璃。
  _effects: flag +factory_callback; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `r13-work.deliver-goods::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/delivered-on-time` tags: commerce, delivery, transport, weather, bribe, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa把十二只木箱码在板车上，用麻绳捆了两道，轮子碾过石子路时，箱角互相磕着响。走到第三道街口，车轴响了一声，Friedrich Lechner蹲下去紧了紧销子。货送到时比约定早半个钟头，店主验完封条，在送货单上画了押。
  _effects: cash 6 (板车上的十二箱); flag +work13_delivered_goods_on_time; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child_

- **1947.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Elisabeth Moser攥着Rosa的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1947.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Alois Lechner提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Josef Winter 2把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Josef Winter 2说不是Rosa的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Rosa心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

- **1947.09** `r13-turn.widowhood::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-pension-queue/workplace-accident` tags: family, widowhood, death, grief, pension, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Winter 2死在工地上，一块掉下来的木梁。工头来通知时，Rosa正在和面，手上的面粉还没擦。她跟着去了工棚，看见Josef Winter 2躺在一张草席上，鞋少了一只。厂里赔了二十克朗，扣掉棺材钱，剩下九克朗。
  _effects: cash 9 (双人床空出的那一半); condition; flag +widowed_by_workplace_accident; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; death (工地木梁坠落)_

  又一年过去，Rosa把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Rosa把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 46 -> 50; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 13 -> 16; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 67 -> 65; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `kernel-family.care-sick-parent::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/care-sick-parent.hired-nurse` tags: task, task:family, family, care, health, parent, work, vienna
  Rosa请Emil Moser 2白天来照看，自己照常上工，每天多付一克朗。Emil Moser 2把Alois Lechner扶到窗边晒太阳，回来时顺便买药；Rosa晚上接班，给Alois Lechner读报纸上的战况和物价，读到一半就有人睡着。读报的声音越来越低，最后只剩炉子里的煤响。
  _effects: cash -10 (daily fee for the neighbour nurse and medicine); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; role; flag +hired_nurse_

- **1948.10** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Rosa挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Anna Lechner 2敲门时，Rosa从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  Rosa在警察局坐了一夜，问话的人换了三个，问题却只有一个：那天集会上站在第几排。天亮时，Helene Stein让Rosa签一份“不再参加非法集会”的保证书。Rosa签了，出门时发现自己的鞋带被剪断了一根，只能拖着走回家。
  _effects: cash -2 (夜里敲门的便衣); condition; flag +questioned_and_released; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

  她本来只是去面包店买盐，被琴声绊住了脚。猴子蹲在帽沿上啃一颗栗子，风琴师脱下礼帽扇风，露出汗湿的额角。曲子完了，她数出两枚铜板放进帽子，听见身后有人吹口哨。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  孤儿院的院长说孩子太多，床不够，让Rosa再等一个月。Berta Weiss 2把Elisabeth 2领回临时收容所，Rosa每周去看一次。孩子渐渐肯说话了，却总是在Rosa起身时抓住衣角，问下一次什么时候来。
  _effects: cash -2 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); condition; flag +waited_for_foster_placement; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008909:child_

- **1949.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Rosa在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Marie Huber从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.03 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-marie-huber_

- **1949.03** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Alois Moser 3手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Alois Moser 3把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Rosa记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1949.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Rosa胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Marie Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Rosa把床单对折，腾出一半，Marie Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012904_

  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Rosa在Villa Schnitzler, Sternwartestraße 71生下一个女婴，取名Elisabeth 2。接生的人收六克朗，Emil Weiss 2在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Rosa起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.05 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:386_

  又一年过去，Rosa把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Rosa把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 50 -> 54; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 16 -> 19; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 65 -> 63; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `kernel-migrate.city-arrival::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-city-arrival-factory` tags: migration, city, work, housing, rural, vienna, urban, postwar
  她在车站把箱子拖下台阶，轮子卡在石缝里。Helene Weiss 2带她去看一间朝院的屋子，说押金可以先欠一半；第二天，她拿着介绍信去Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，门房让她在门口等到午休，最后收下她做临时工。
  _effects: employment Fabrikarbeiter @ org-winter-print; residence; cash -3 (押金、车钱和第一周的饭钱（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; flag +arrived_in_city; knowledge “工厂门房会让人等到午休再决定是否收临时工，押金可以商量”_

- **1950.03** `r2-law.savings-passbook::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/smudged-deposit` tags: finance, bank, savings, household, contract, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa把婚礼剩下的钱存进储蓄银行，Alois Berg在蓝格子里写下数目，墨水洇开一小块。Rosa让Alois Berg重写，Alois Berg说存折不能换页，洇开的部分由储户自己负责。Emil Weiss 2在门口等，问利息够不够买一只新锅。
  _effects: cash -8 (存折上的蓝格子（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +deposited_wedding_savings; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; knowledge “the savings bank will not replace a smudged passbook page”_

- **1950.08** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1950.09** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1950.12** `r13-rel.class-difference::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::class-difference-crisis/refused-the-name` tags: relationship, courtship, class, money, marriage, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil Weiss 2的父亲托Luise Kral 3带话，只要Rosa肯入赘并把姓改掉，就替Rosa还清欠租。Rosa在桌前坐到天黑，最后把笔放下，说自己可以穷，但不能连名字都没有。

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Rosa不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Rosa拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Rosa的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

  _effects: cash -1 (失业救济的号码牌); mood -0.12; condition; flag +benefit_denied; knowledge “a voluntary departure disqualifies the claim”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

  Rosa把Katharina Hofer和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Rosa把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Luise Kral 3把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Rosa去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

  _effects: flag +cast_as_understudy; role; skill; knowledge “the understudy must know the whole second act by Friday”_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: condition; flag +conversion_postponed_

  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  Franz Moser在楼道里拦住Rosa，问家里那张盖红章的纸是不是出了事。Rosa说只是旧房租收据，回家却把纸从柜底翻出来，和Friedrich Lechner一起塞进米缸夹层。两人把米重新铺平，Friedrich Lechner的手一直在抖；Rosa说这事到此为止，谁也别再提。

  Rosa记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Emil Moser 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Emil Moser 2陪着Rosa去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。

  Rosa在院子里的水管前排了半个钟头，前面的Elisabeth Kraus忽然把桶挪开，让Rosa先接。Rosa说不用，Elisabeth Kraus说你的孩子还在哭。两人就这样认识了；后来Elisabeth Kraus替Rosa看孩子，Rosa替Elisabeth Kraus排队买煤，谁也没有说过“帮忙”两个字。
  _effects: flag +friendship_at_the_water_tap; role; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001253:child_

  Rosa的抚恤金要等再婚满一年才停，Josef Winter 3说那就先不办手续。两人住在一起，对外说是亲戚。办事处来查过一次，孩子在门口说这是她叔叔。

  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment 细木学徒 @ org-winter-print; skill; relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  Anton在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Anton的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1933.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.work-injury/union-rep` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Grete Stein 2把Anton领到工会代表面前，说护罩少了螺丝不是头一回。代表翻开小本记下机器编号，答应陪Anton去医院，费用由工会垫付，但要Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故单上签字，工头磨蹭半天才落笔。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (工会垫付后自理的零头); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时就医处理伤口); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; flag +union_contact; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1933.11** `major-crisis.press-accident/press-kills` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health
  同一台机器在夜班又卡住了。Anton伸手去拽纸边，滚筒突然合拢，把人和半截工作服一起拖向齿轮箱。Grete Stein 2拉下总闸时，Anton的胸口已经塌下去一块。医生蹲在油污的地上写死亡证明，工厂当天下午就换了新的防护栏，工头说订单不能停。
  _effects: death (crushed chest in a printing press)_

  _effects: cash 2 (放学后的四个钟头); flag +work13_took_part_time_errands; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; employment Laufbursche @ org-die-fackel; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1926.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/borrow-notes-at-home` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  学费凑不出来，Hedwig就向同屋的学徒借笔记。晚上把灶台边的桌子擦干净，用铅笔头把讲义抄在旧账单背面，Ferdinand Lechner允他每两周交一次抄本抵作练习。抄到第三页时灯芯烧短了，字迹开始发颤。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1926.10** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::ferris-wheel-reform/tested-the-new-brake` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, reform, medical, schooling
  游乐场新装了紧急制动，铁牌上写着“由市议会验讫”。摩天轮升到一半，Friedrich Stein拉了一下闸，座舱猛地停住，又慢慢往下走。Hedwig问这是不是坏了，Friedrich Stein说这是新规矩，每趟都要试一次。Friedrich Lechner在座位上把栗子撒了一地。
  _effects: mood -0.03; condition; flag +tested_new_ferris_brake; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “the municipal inspector requires a brake test on every ride”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1928.01** `agency:request_help` tags: agency, agency:request_help
  Hedwig去找Josefine Hofer求助，对方也正被房租和病人拖住，只能摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/works-in-kitchen-for-board` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig交不起住宿费，校长让Hedwig每天清早去厨房帮工抵账。Hedwig四点起来削土豆，手上冻出裂口，上课时打瞌睡被Josef Weiss点名。期末，Hedwig的操行栏写着“勤勉”，成绩却掉到中游。第二学期Hedwig的操行还是“勤勉”，成绩单寄回家，家里只看了学费那栏。
  _effects: cash -3 (寄宿学校的铁皮箱（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1930.01** `kernel-health.task-tuberculosis-cure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tb-waiting-list` tags: health, task, tuberculosis, sanatorium, cure, reform, medical, schooling
  床位要等一年，Hedwig先回家。白天咳得少了就做点糊盒活，晚上把痰吐进一只带盖的杯子；Otto Huber送来一瓶鱼肝油，说山里的空气不要钱，可惜搬不去。杯子每天煮一次，杯沿还是有一圈洗不掉的印子。
  _effects: cash -5 (cod-liver oil and a covered sputum cup（其中5克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; knowledge “疗养院床位要等一年；在家只能把痰吐进带盖的杯子，靠鱼肝油和糊盒活撑着。”_

- **1930.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Friedrich Hofer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Berta Weiss 2在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Hedwig，Friedrich Hofer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1930.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Otto Huber在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Otto Huber敲开Hedwig的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1930.12** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Berta Kral一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

  Gustav Weiss病倒以后，楼里开始有人避开Hedwig。没有人说是诅咒，但每个人都在数Hedwig最近和谁说过话。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; flag +curse_suspected; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/semmel-no-words` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel（其中0.14克朗记入债务）); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.03_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-depression/leaves-vienna` tags: adulthood, depression, work, migration, unemployment
  Hedwig听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig请楼下的Elisabeth Lechner白天来照看Josefine Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Elisabeth Lechner上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Hedwig下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Josefine Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.join-second-row` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Hedwig把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Anna Stein在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Hedwig站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。
  _effects: cash -2 (合唱团月费和铜徽章钱); mood 0.07; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1932.02** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Hedwig托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Hedwig晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Hedwig却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Hedwig把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Gustav Weiss提着空碗上来时，只看见Hedwig把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  Hedwig还不上旧账，只好向Josef Berg介绍的放贷人再借十克朗，月息两成。Anna Lechner 3收了旧账，又重新开了一页赊账；Hedwig在两本账上都按了手印。回家路上，Hedwig把两张借据分开藏，怕家里人看见。
  _effects: cash 10 (借新债还旧账); flag +new_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; knowledge “放贷人的月息是两成，借据要分开藏。”; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1933.08** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  菜贩玛丽亚把账记在木牌上，Hedwig答应周五还。到了周五，Hedwig把三个克朗数了又数，终于递过去，Friedrich Hofer在旁边看着。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账); food_

  Gustav Weiss把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.049999999999999996; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold_

  医生到的时候天已经黑了。他摸了摸额头，说准备后事，收了出诊费就走；Friedrich Hofer不肯走，用湿布擦了一夜。天亮时Hedwig退了烧，枕头湿了一大片。邻居把账单压在钟底下，继续擦身，直到天亮。
  _effects: cash -8 (house call fee paid even though the doctor gave no treatment（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.019999999999999997 (fever broke after a night of cold compresses); condition; knowledge “医生说“准备后事”以后，邻居用湿布擦了一夜，烧自己退了。”_

  Hedwig接到传球时，泥水已经没到脚踝。Josef在看台下面喊“别停”，Maria Winter的后卫伸脚来铲，鞋钉先碰到球，再碰到Hedwig的小腿。Alois Berg的哨子含在嘴里，一直没有响。Hedwig爬起来，把球捅进网里，球网是补过的，破洞里露出后面的土豆地。

  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Hedwig把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Anna Bauer站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Hedwig手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。

  _effects: cash 20 (舅舅借出的钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; flag +owes_uncle_

  那孩子一边跑一边吹口哨，狗就跟着口哨声跑。Hedwig追到街角时，罗盘钟正敲整点。她扶着墙喘，低头看见鞋底磨穿的洞，算着补鞋匠那里补一只要收六十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (补鞋底的开销（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  Hedwig喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Friedrich Hofer在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Hedwig替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Hedwig自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Rosa在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Hedwig排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/violets-on-the-step` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1939.08** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Hedwig走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Hedwig挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Hedwig侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.06; skill_

- **1940.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-pot-empty` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  前面还有七八个人时，Alois Berg已经直起腰把锅盖扣上了。队伍没散，有人骂了一句，有人蹲下把罐子收进布袋。Hedwig走到巷口，Elisabeth Lechner从后面赶上来，罐子还温着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1941.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.ask-fare` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Hedwig向Ferdinand Hofer问清下个月郊外演唱会的车费由谁出，Ferdinand Hofer说团里只贴一半，另一半要团员自己凑。Hedwig把四十赫勒硬币在指间转了两圈，最终没有放进收款盒，说等发了工钱再来；这一来一去，也算清了半票、酒棚和车钱各是多少。出门时Josef Bauer 2正扛着啤酒桶进后厅，两人侧身让过。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1941.06** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-fee-lost` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  一克朗是Hedwig身上最后一张整票。纸条上的地址在城郊，坐电车又花了几赫勒。工头叼着烟说你来晚了，Hedwig攥着纸条站了一会儿，回程的路走了一个钟头。
  _effects: cash -1 (职业介绍所登记费); mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1941.06** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.fish-heads` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  鱼头用报纸包着，报纸上还印着昨天的广告。Ferdinand Lechner一边刮冰一边说，回去多搁姜，别舍不得醋。Hedwig把纸包拎在手里，能感觉到凉气从指缝里钻出来，走到家时报纸已经洇湿了一层。
  _effects: food; health 0.020000000000000018 (喝上了热鱼汤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1942.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/night-watch` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Hedwig整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Josefine Hofer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Hedwig在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (整夜未睡，受凉); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; cash -1.2 (迟到被扣的半天工钱)_

- **1942.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Rosa Huber的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Rosa Huber提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1942.05** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  六个十字币换来一条鳟鱼和一把欧芹。Hedwig把鱼煎得两面焦黄，鱼尾巴给了闻着味来的Gustav Weiss。自家孩子用面包把盘底的黄油擦干净。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009172_

- **1943.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.first-winery` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Hedwig和Elisabeth Bauer在第一个酒棚就坐下了，要了一壶新酒，Alois Moser 3摸出一副磨损的纸牌。三人打到下午，太阳偏西也没再往上爬；Hedwig输了几个铜板，连酒带牌局一共一克朗五十赫勒，倒赢回一肚子笑话。回程的电车上，Hedwig靠着窗，酒意上来，差点坐过站。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (酒棚的新酒和牌局输的铜板); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1943.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Friedrich Lechner在午休时把本子摊在Hedwig的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Hedwig从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Friedrich Lechner当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child; reputation 0.04_

- **1943.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig看Anton Weiss盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Anton Weiss愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Hedwig门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Hedwig打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: food_

- **1943.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Rosa Moser问是谁动的。Rosa Moser说是Anton Weiss嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1944.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Hedwig和Berta Weiss 2在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Berta Weiss 2把煤钱推过来，Hedwig接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.01** `r13-fam.adopt-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/adoption-granted` tags: task, family, adoption, child, law, household, vienna, urban
  Alois Berg把表格翻到最后一页，让Hedwig和Berta Hofer都在上面按手印。孩子的手指太小，按了两次才清楚；Alois Berg在姓氏栏里写下Hedwig的姓，又盖了一个蓝章。走出办事处时，Berta Hofer问以后是不是要改口，Hedwig说想叫什么就叫什么。
  _effects: cash -8 (登记簿上的新姓氏); flag +adoption_granted; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006531:child; knowledge “the court granted the adoption and changed the child’s surna”; household_

- **1944.10** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/bombed-out` tags: war, homefront, shortage, migration, injury
  半夜的空袭把屋顶掀开一角。Hedwig和邻居把家具拖到楼道，天亮后去市政办事处登记损失。窗户用木板钉上，一家人挤到地下室。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; health -0.08000000000000002 (air raid and cold); condition; cash -6 (repairs and moving)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration, injury
  炮火把Hedwig住的那条街烧掉一半。Hedwig跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; health -0.07999999999999996 (displacement and cold); cash -5 (temporary shelter)_

- **1945.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::foster-postwar/foster-from-orphanage` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  孤儿院门口排着领孩子的队伍，Anna Bauer把Grete Kral推到Hedwig面前，说这孩子夜里不哭，只是不肯吃饭。Hedwig蹲下来，把带来的半块糖放在孩子手心；孩子没有吃，先把糖纸抹平，夹进衣袋。那天下午，Hedwig在登记簿上按了手印。
  _effects: cash -4 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); flag +fostered_postwar_orphan; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008220:child; knowledge “the child hoards smooth paper and sugar wrappers”; household_

- **1945.08** `major-crisis.rent-increase/locks-changed` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, migration, injury
  第二个星期，Hedwig下工回来，门锁已经换了。床架、锅和一只装票据的铁盒堆在楼道里，看门人Berta Huber把一张写有“自愿迁出”的纸递过来，说签了就能把东西搬进后院的共用间。Hedwig没有签，先把铁盒抱进怀里，手指被箱角划出一道口子。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; cash -9 (cart, storage and a replacement lock); condition; health -0.020000000000000018 (cut hand and a night in the corridor); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002399:child; role; flag +evicted_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010572_

- **1946.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refuses-the-money` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Josefine Hofer把钱推过来，Hedwig又推回去。Hedwig说自己只是倒霉，不是什么会看事的人。Josefine Hofer把钱留在桌上走了，第二天又派人来取。Hedwig把那一夜省下来的蜡烛收进柜子，没有点过。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; flag +refused_curse_work; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Hedwig下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; skill_

- **1946.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/help-refused` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, vienna, urban, postwar
  Franz Stein把煤筐往身后挪，说不用，自己搬得动。Hedwig站在楼梯转角，听见对方上一层歇一层，筐底在墙上刮出长长的白印。晚饭时，Hedwig把自家的汤盛了一碗，放在Franz Stein门口，没有敲门。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +left_soup_at_neighbour_door; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1946.05** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::liberation-anniversary/cleared-of-collaboration` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, postwar
  解放一周年，广场上立了一块临时木牌，写着“感谢”。Hedwig发现自己的名字被刻在“合作者”那一栏，因为战时在占领军厨房干过活。Anna Bauer替Hedwig作证，说Hedwig往汤里多兑了水，救过不少人；Helene Stein听完，让人把木牌上的字刨掉。
  _effects: mood 0.03999999999999998; reputation 0.08; flag +cleared_of_collaboration_charge; relation friend with person-anna-bauer; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; knowledge “my name was carved under collaborators”_

- **1946.09** `r3-war.mia-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-mia-clarification/found-unidentified-burial-record` tags: war, missing, bureaucracy, family, grief, vienna, urban, postwar
  战后清理档案，Helene Stein找到一张模糊的埋葬记录，上面写着“无名，步兵，1916年7月”。Hedwig问是不是自己的兄弟，Helene Stein说不能确定；Marie Huber建议去公墓看编号。那天下午，Hedwig在编号前站到关门。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +found_unidentified_burial_record; role; knowledge “an unknown infantryman was buried in July 1916”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1947.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/quiet-mercy` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Emil Bauer病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Emil Bauer走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; flag +curse_mercy; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010561_

  Katharina Lechner站在走廊尽头，脚边一只用绳子捆住的纸箱，问能不能借宿两晚。Hedwig把厨房的折叠床打开，又烧了一壶水。夜里，Katharina Lechner在梦里说了一种她听不懂的话，Hedwig把门闩又检查了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (走廊里的陌生口音); food; flag +sheltered_refugee_two_nights; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1947.05** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1947.06** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Hedwig的裙角。Rosa Winter的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Hedwig咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001487:child; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

- **1947.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1947.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1947.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1948.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Mitzi忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Hedwig陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Mitzi站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1948.04** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Hedwig去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1948.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Katharina Hofer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Katharina Hofer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1948.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Anton Hofer 2手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Anton Hofer 2把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Hedwig记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

- **1949.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/quiet-mercy` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Alois Lechner病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Alois Lechner走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/violets-on-the-step` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰); relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1949.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (鞋底不平磨出水泡); skill_

- **1950.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Helene Weiss请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Helene Weiss把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition_

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.help-refugee::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sheltered-two-nights` tags: social, refugee, shelter, war, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Kraus站在走廊尽头，脚边一只用绳子捆住的纸箱，问能不能借宿两晚。Hedwig把厨房的折叠床打开，又烧了一壶水。夜里，Hermann Kraus在梦里说了一种她听不懂的话，Hedwig把门闩又检查了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (走廊里的陌生口音); food; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003200:child_

- **1950.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Hedwig把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1950.12** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/tells-the-priest` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:383; reputation -0.020000000000000004; condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 30 -> 40; reputation -0.04; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1951.01** `r13-work.open-shop::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-vacant-shop/lease-dispute` tags: commerce, shop, capital, licence, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Stein先答应租，等Hedwig把货搬进去，又说得加一笔“门面修缮费”。Hedwig把口头约定复述了一遍，Maria Stein说没有纸就不算。货堆在走廊里过了一夜，第二天Hedwig把最贵的两箱搬回去，剩下的折价卖给了对街的Josef Bauer 2。
  _effects: cash -5 (卷帘门上的第一块木牌); flag +work13_shop_lease_dispute; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1951.02** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  负责人说这是不信任，Hedwig回说数目对得上才叫信任。Anna Kraus把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: reputation 0.039999999999999994; skill_

- **1951.08** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Hedwig用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Alois Moser 4翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Josef Moser 2在走廊里问交了多少，Hedwig含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1951.10** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Luise Kral 3用膝盖顶住，Hedwig把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.050000000000000044 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛)_

- **1952.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/money-closes-the-file` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, vienna, urban
  Rosa Hofer 2托人带话，说只要Hedwig出钱修好教堂的侧门，证词就可以当作没有写过。Hedwig把钱数了三遍，最后交出去。侧门修好了，Rosa Hofer 2在门框上刻了一行小字，刻的是Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: cash -3 (修教堂侧门的封口钱); state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 25 -> 10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; reputation -0.02; flag +paid_to_close_accusation; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 10 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Ferdinand Lechner扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Hedwig把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149_

- **1952.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Anton Hofer 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anton Hofer 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.10** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Hedwig把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Rosa Hofer递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1953.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/quiet-mercy` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Anton Weiss 2病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Anton Weiss 2走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 2 -> 0; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Grete Stein 2忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Hedwig陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Grete Stein 2站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1953.07** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Josef Hofer在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1954.01** `statistical_death/accident` tags: death
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang附近出了事故。工友和路人把人抬到门口，等医生赶到时已经没有了呼吸。Berta Hofer在场。
  _effects: death (意外事故)_
